local function rws_epv(gtb_epw)local qsn_epx if gtb_epw%2==0 then qsn_epx=math.floor(gtb_epw/2)else qsn_epx=3*gtb_epw+1 end return qsn_epx>=1 end local hkt_epy=984 if rws_epv(hkt_epy)then local mqo_epz=6204 end end do local function qkd_eqa(xgu_eqb,uah_eqc)local akh_eqd=bit32.bxor(xgu_eqb,uah_eqc)local ktm_eqe=bit32.bxor(akh_eqd,uah_eqc)local hco_eqf=bit32.bxor(ktm_eqe,0)return hco_eqf==xgu_eqb end local cww_eqg=40 local zwo_eqh=97 if qkd_eqa(cww_eqg,zwo_eqh)then local kyu_eqi=2499 end end do local function awv_eqj(eau_eqk)local bwb_eql=eau_eqk*eau_eqk local plx_eqm=(bwb_eql+eau_eqk)%2 return plx_eqm==0 end local gwg_eqn=276 if awv_eqj(gwg_eqn)then local ind_eqo=5762 end end do local function ube_eqp(omf_eqq,ari_eqr,mcn_eqs,yzo_eqt)local dis_equ=omf_eqq+ari_eqr local jsl_eqv=dis_equ-ari_eqr+mcn_eqs local oto_eqw=jsl_eqv*yzo_eqt local lwx_eqx=math.floor(oto_eqw/yzo_eqt)return lwx_eqx-mcn_eqs==omf_eqq end local tdk_eqy=3915 if
